--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
MODEL: MeasurementItem
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field                  | Type                  | Presence | Meaning / validation
  -----------------------|-----------------------|----------|-------------------------------
  local_uuid             | UUID                  | required | Stable observation upload identity
  tree_id                | UUID                  | required | Same-campus stable tree
  plot_id                | UUID                  | required | Plot in survey's exact plot set
  measured_at            | timestamp             | required | Actual measurement time
  location               | GeoJSON Point or NULL | required | Actual captured location; NULL draft requires GPS_NOT_CAPTURED
  gps_accuracy_m         | Decimal or NULL       | required | >=0 when captured; NULL with missing GPS, never fabricated as zero
  dbh_cm                 | Decimal or NULL       | required | >0 when measured/derived
  height_m               | Decimal or NULL       | required | >0 when supported
  height_source          | TEXT                  | required | AR, FIELD_MEASURED, APPROVED_MODEL or UNKNOWN
  species_code           | TEXT or NULL          | required | Known supported catalog code
  species_selection      | TEXT                  | required | CLASSIFIER, MANUAL, EXPERT or UNKNOWN
  species_confidence     | Decimal or NULL       | required | [0,1]
  ar_tier                | INTEGER               | required | 1, 2 or 3
  allometry_id           | UUID or NULL          | required | Approved applicable method parameter
  wood_density_source_id | UUID or NULL          | required | Approved source parameter
  measurement_height_m   | Decimal               | required | >0
  protocol_version       | UUID                  | required | Exact published measurement protocol content ID
  measurement_form       | TEXT                  | required | DIAMETER, CIRCUMFERENCE or MULTISTEM
  circumference_cm       | Decimal or NULL       | required | >0 for circumference input
  stems                  | MeasurementStem[]     | required | [] except declared supported multistem input
  evidence_ids           | UUID[]                | required | Mapped READY evidence identities
  capture_quality        | CaptureQuality        | required | Device/model/GPS provenance
  client_content_hash    | SHA-256               | required | MCJ1 of complete write item excluding this field

Validation / behavior:
  - Partial dimensions/species may remain honest drafts; missing eligibility blocks
    submit/biomass use.
  - CIRCUMFERENCE preserves the entered value; server derives DBH. MULTISTEM requires
    a reviewed protocol, not an invented equivalent diameter.
  - DIAMETER requires reviewed DBH; CIRCUMFERENCE preserves entered circumference
      and backend derives DBH; MULTISTEM requires reviewed protocol/allometry or
      NEEDS_DATA.
  - No guessed equivalent diameter or unknown-stem aggregation.

  ar_tier                      | INTEGER                        | required | 1, 2 or 3; agrees with MeasurementItem.ar_tier
  classifier_version           | TEXT or NULL                   | required | Actual species model version
  height_provider_version      | TEXT or NULL                   | required | Actual provider/model version
  capture_flags                | TEXT[]                         | required | Approved policy flags; not an eligibility bypass
  measurement_uncertainty      | MeasurementUncertainty or NULL | required | Only sourced bounds; unknown stays NULL
  gps_recorded_at              | timestamp or NULL              | required | Actual GPS time; NULL with GPS_NOT_CAPTURED
  consent_context_id           | TEXT or NULL                   | required | Applicable consent reference

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

MODEL: MeasurementBatch
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field               | Type              | Presence | Meaning / validation
  --------------------|-------------------|----------|-------------------------------
  items               | MeasurementItem[] | required | 1–200 observations
  survey_revision     | INTEGER           | required | Survey BUSINESS revision; not input_revision
  client_operation_id | UUID              | required | Equals Idempotency-Key for the batch

Validation / behavior:
  - If-Match is the survey input_revision. Structural/content conflicts reject the
    entire batch; honest partial-quality rows can save with issues.
  - survey_revision equals the survey's BUSINESS revision; it is not
      input_revision.
  - Both must match the exact survey ID. client_operation_id is the batch
      operation identity and equals Idempotency-Key; individual local_uuid
      identifies observation.

  location               | GeoJSON Point or NULL | optional | Actual captured location; NULL draft requires GPS_NOT_CAPTURED
  gps_accuracy_m         | Decimal or NULL       | optional | >=0 when captured; NULL with missing GPS, never fabricated as zero
  dbh_cm                 | Decimal or NULL       | optional | >0 when measured/derived
  height_m               | Decimal or NULL       | optional | >0 when supported
  height_source          | TEXT                  | optional | AR, FIELD_MEASURED, APPROVED_MODEL or UNKNOWN
  species_code           | TEXT or NULL          | optional | Known supported catalog code
  species_selection      | TEXT                  | optional | CLASSIFIER, MANUAL, EXPERT or UNKNOWN
  species_confidence     | Decimal or NULL       | optional | [0,1]

  wood_density_source_id | UUID or NULL          | optional | Approved source parameter

  client_operation_id    | UUID                  | required | Fresh mutation ID; equals Idempotency-Key
  client_content_hash    | SHA-256               | required | MCJ1 of the full merged MeasurementItem excluding its hash

Validation / behavior:
  - Require at least one changed observation field. local_uuid and tree_id cannot
    change.
  - Merge with the original saved write item, then hash the complete merged item.
    Exclude client_operation_id, response metadata and local base_survey_revision from
    MCJ1.

  method_version | UUID         | optional | Exact approved accounting method
  coverage       | Coverage     | optional | Stated operational inventory boundary
  denominators   | Denominators | optional | Unknown facts remain NULL

Validation / behavior:
  - At least one change. Period/timezone may change only in the first unsubmitted
    family revision; 18.11 defines the immutable period rule.

    completeness checks run before independent approval or activation.

MODEL: RegistryResource
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Extends: ResourceMeta (all named inherited fields; no extra database columns).

Fields:

  Field               | Type                                  | Presence | Meaning / validation
  --------------------|---------------------------------------|----------|-------------------------------
  kind                | TEXT                                  | required | FACTOR, METHOD, MODEL or KNOWLEDGE; must match endpoint
  logical_key         | TEXT                                  | required | 1..120 characters
  version             | INTEGER                               | required | >=1; new version for a published correction
  schema_version      | INTEGER                               | required | 1
  title               | TEXT                                  | required | 1..200 characters
  publisher           | TEXT                                  | required | Actual source publisher
  source_url          | HTTPS URL or NULL                     | required | Approved source location
  source_evidence_id  | UUID or NULL                          | required | READY source bytes required before publication
  source_version      | TEXT                                  | required | Exact cited source version
  published_date      | DATE or NULL                          | required | Source publication date
  license             | TEXT                                  | required | Reviewed usage conditions
  source_page         | TEXT or NULL                          | required | Relevant page
  source_section      | TEXT or NULL                          | required | Relevant section
  geography_codes     | TEXT[]                                | required | Applicable geography codes
  valid_from          | DATE or NULL                          | required | Applicability start
  valid_to            | DATE or NULL                          | required | Applicability end
  applicability_notes | TEXT                                  | required | Source limits and assumptions
  config              | DraftRegistryConfig or RegistryConfig | required | DRAFT returns typed draft content; APPROVED/ACTIVE returns a complete validated config
  status              | RegistryStatus                        | required | Immutable published versions
  artifact_hash       | SHA-256                               | required | Current canonical metadata/config content hash
  author_id           | UUID                                  | required | Registry author
  reviewer_id         | UUID or NULL                          | required | Independent reviewer
  validated_at        | timestamp or NULL                     | required | Validation time

Validation / behavior:
  - Common metadata id/counter/timestamps/origin comes from ResourceMeta;
    schema_version has the same literal value 1, not a second nested key. Global
    registry resources omit institution_id.
  - Public content artifact_hash is derived from the reviewed metadata/config and
    source checksum. Never expose a provider credential or raw storage path.

    model/factor values are returned.

MODEL: SurveyOfflineContext
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field                  | Type       | Presence | Meaning / validation
  -----------------------|------------|----------|-------------------------------

  institution_id         | UUID       | required | Authorized tenant
  permission             | Permission | required | OWNER or MANAGER at grant time

  survey_id              | UUID       | required | Requested exact DRAFT survey revision
  boundary_id            | UUID       | required | Frozen grant geometry

Validation / behavior:
  - Map issued_at/expires_at to authorization_checked_at/offline_context_expires_at
    described in 21.2. The grant is cached authority for bounded local fieldwork,
    never a bearer credential or a replacement for server membership checks.
  - Record Android boot/elapsed time at verified receipt; local expiry is the smaller
    of server remaining time and 24h. Reboot, explicit denial or demo generation
    change invalidates it.

MODEL: ImportResource

  Field                    | Type            | Presence | Meaning / validation
  -------------------------|-----------------|----------|-------------------------------

  expires_at               | timestamp       | required | Exact commit deadline
  input_revision           | INTEGER         | required | Parent counter on which preview was based

    Cursor binds normalized_hash/status. Viewing a committed preview does not commit
    it again.

MODEL: AnalyticsQuery

Fields:

  Field        | Type         | Presence | Meaning / validation
  -------------|--------------|----------|-------------------------------
  audit_ids    | UUID[]       | optional | <=24 authorized business revision IDs
  period_start | DATE         | optional | Inclusive reporting-date filter
  period_end   | DATE         | optional | >=period_start; selected span <=5 years
  campus_ids   | UUID[]       | optional | <=200 authorized selected campuses

  category     | TEXT         | optional | One supported emission category

  metric       | TEXT         | optional | actual_total, tree_stock, removal, release, inventory_balance or avoided_total

    periods (up to five), plus the current operational draft summary for OWNER/MANAGER
    only.
  - VIEWER selection is restricted to released FINALIZED/SUPERSEDED snapshots.
    Uncalculated draft quantities remain NULL with explicit limitations.

  Field               | Type                       | Presence | Meaning / validation
  --------------------|----------------------------|----------|-------------------------------
  audit_id            | UUID                       | required | Exact authorized business revision
  snapshot_id         | UUID or NULL               | required | NULL only for an uncalculated OWNER/MANAGER draft
  revision            | INTEGER                    | required | Business revision
  period_start        | DATE                       | required | Inclusive local start
  period_end          | DATE                       | required | Inclusive local end
  timezone            | TEXT                       | required | Reporting timezone
  coverage            | Coverage                   | required | Frozen stated boundary
  actual_total        | Decimal or NULL            | required | Covered actual emissions tCO2e
  by_scope            | map<Scope,Decimal or NULL> | required | Actual tCO2e by scope
  by_category         | map<TEXT,Decimal or NULL>  | required | Actual tCO2e by category
  tree_stock          | Decimal or NULL            | required | Standing tCO2, not annual removal
  removal             | Decimal or NULL            | required | Attributed period tCO2
  release             | Decimal or NULL            | required | Attributed period tCO2
  avoided_by_action   | map<TEXT,Decimal or NULL>  | required | Separate project benefits tCO2e
  inventory_balance   | Decimal or NULL            | required | Removal minus actual emissions and release
  intensities         | PeriodIntensities          | required | tCO2e per explicitly recorded denominator
  comparability_flags | TEXT[]                     | required | Period/coverage/method/factor/stock limitations

  floor_area | Decimal or NULL | required | Actual tCO2e per recorded square metre

  original_http_status | INTEGER      | conditional | Receipt-expiry recovery only

  mutation_committed | BOOLEAN | conditional | Receipt expiry only; literal true for a matched committed tombstone
  accepted_revision | INTEGER or NULL | conditional | Receipt expiry only; original acknowledged parent counter
  recovery_kind | TEXT | conditional | RESOURCE_GET, DELETE_ACK, ACK_ONLY or SECRET_REISSUE

Validation / behavior:
  - Error.details is NULL for other codes. Domain mismatch has exactly the two domain
    keys. Receipt expiry has exactly the five recovery fields in this model. Do not expose another
    actor, tenant or raw response.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field                      | Type   | Presence | Meaning / validation
  ---------------------------|--------|----------|-------------------------------
  resource                   | object | required | Exact frozen resource selected by case resource_type below
  contributor_user_ids       | UUID[] | required | Frozen source/editor/submitter contributors
  historical_member_user_ids | UUID[] | required | Members during the reviewed work, for independence checks

Resource discriminator:
  REGISTRATION -> RegistrationResource; CAMPUS_CONTROL -> CampusResource;
  BOUNDARY -> BoundaryResource; MASK -> MaskResource; PLOT_SET -> PlotSetResource;
  SURVEY -> SurveyResource; TREE_AUDIT -> TreeAuditResource;
  INSTITUTION_AUDIT -> AuditResource plus its frozen input/calculation snapshot.
  The case's authorized detail projection includes its scoped evidence only.
  These actor/claim metadata fields do not alter the numerical result hash.

  Field                     | Type                 | Presence | Meaning / validation
  --------------------------|----------------------|----------|-------------------------------

  revision                  | INTEGER              | required | Plot-set revision
  design                    | QualitySampling      | required | Exact saved approved generation design
  review_status             | GeometryReview       | required | Candidate is not approved automatically
  input_hash                | SHA-256              | required | Frozen design and generated plot content
  generation_job_id         | UUID                 | required | Persisted producer job
  correction_of_plot_set_id | UUID or NULL         | required | Prior immutable set when correcting
  plot_count                | INTEGER              | required | Saved plot count; page plots separately
  review_reason             | TEXT or NULL         | required | Independent review explanation

WIRE-TO-STORAGE MAPPINGS
-------------------------
  Public field / model                 | Physical storage / derivation
  -------------------------------------|------------------------------------------
  CalculationLine.contribution_unit    | institution_calculation_lines.result_unit
  CalculationLine.contribution_amount  | Snapshot's normalized line contribution
  RegistryResource.artifact_hash       | Canonical registry metadata/config/source hash
  RegistrationResource.input_hash      | Current canonical saved draft content hash
  RegistrationResource.frozen_input_hash | Frozen submitted registration content hash
  ResourceMeta.schema_version          | Literal wire version 1 when not stored separately
  Page.next_cursor                     | Server-issued signed/opaque paging state
  ResultResource.versions              | Frozen calculation version manifest, not live joins
  CaptureParameter.formula              | method_parameters.formula_key
  MeasurementResource raw write fields  | campus_tree_measurements.raw_input
  MeasurementResource.derivation       | derived_dbh_cm + validation_status/issues
  EvidenceResource.bytes_available      | READY + live verified bytes + no purge marker
  ExportResource.bytes_available        | READY artifact + live bytes + no purge marker
  ExportResource.download_artifact_id  | Compatibility alias equal to artifact_id

Draft registration saves recompute input_hash in the same transaction as its
counter. A nullable staging column is never returned as a missing current hash.
Frozen hash stays null until submission; later correction creates a new snapshot.
Repositories return domain data; serializers apply these mappings explicitly.

18.7 ACTIVITY DETAILS, CSV AND MEASUREMENT MUTATION RULES
--------------------------------------------------------
Activity schema ownership:
  - BSDD 18.11 defines exact EmissionLine.details and MitigationLine baseline/
    project/attributes keys, scalar types, conditions, units and allocation rules.
  - METHOD.activity_schemas must implement those versioned variants.
  - Formula semantics and factor provenance remain in 17.3–17.8.
  - No alternate keys such as energy_intensity_kWh_per_kL, feedstock_ids or
    treatment_method_id are aliases.
  - Project component IDs resolve to actual same-audit/same-period rows; backend
    derives project totals.
  - Null draft data stays unknown, not invented zero.

Measurement mutations:
  - POST batch inserts new local UUIDs or returns matching existing observations.
  - Different bytes for an existing local UUID -> 409 MEASUREMENT_CONTENT_CONFLICT.
  - PUT /tree-surveys/{sid}/measurements/{mid} updates an unfrozen draft with
    If-Match + a fresh client_operation_id; keep an edit audit event.
  - DELETE that draft measurement requires If-Match + idempotency key and returns
    the new survey input_revision.
  - No accepted observation is hard-deleted.
  - Accepted correction uses a NEW survey revision; copy selected observations and
    replace the corrected logical tree observation in the new snapshot only.
  - Batch structural/conflict errors return field_errors and insert none.
  - Valid partial-quality observations can be saved with validation issues; quality
    issues block submit/biomass eligibility, not honest draft capture.
  - Same-item batch retry is resolved before If-Match rejection, after current
    authorization, and returns existing dispositions without a second insert.
  - Other concurrent edits require full revision conflict reconciliation.

CSV template — initial delivery imports emission activity only:
  GET /institution-audits/{aid}/imports/template?category=... returns protected
    UTF-8 template (or template artifact download) and schema_version=1.
  Columns: line_id,category,subcategory,campus_id,source_id,activity_start,
    activity_end,quantity,unit,source_type,estimate_notes,evidence_ids,
    included_in_line_id,allocation_ratio,details_json.
  line_id may be blank for new rows; server assigns ID during frozen preview.
  Dates ISO; quantity uses '.' decimal without locale thousands separators.
  evidence_ids is a JSON UUID array; details_json is the category object above.
  UTF-8 with optional BOM; comma delimiter; quoted fields per RFC4180.
  Other delimiters require explicit mapping; never guess and silently drop columns.
  No XLSX, mitigation nested import or bill OCR is implied by this CSV feature.

ImportPreview request:
  evidence_id UUID (READY CSV under this audit), category, column_mapping object,
  expected_input_revision INTEGER, operation APPEND_NEW.
ImportPreview result:
  preview_id,expires_at,input_revision,file_hash,normalized_hash,
  rows Page<{row_number,line_id,normalized_line,errors[],warnings[]}>;
  first page defaults 25, maximum 100; subsequent rows use the import GET route;
  valid_count,error_count,duplicate_count,can_commit BOOLEAN.
ImportCommit request:
  preview_id,normalized_hash,expected_input_revision; no editable rows in commit.
ImportCommit result:
  batch_id=preview_id,status=COMMITTED,created_count,input_revision.
  Created line IDs/rows are paged through the import GET route, not an unbounded
  10000-row response. The batch keeps its immutable normalization/commit manifest.
  - Initial commit is APPEND_NEW only; existing line_id/source/date duplicates fail.
  - Correct imported rows later through normal draft category replacement.
  - Preview expires after 30 minutes; file/mapping/audit revision change invalidates it.
  - Commit locks the audit, checks all rows/evidence/overlap, then inserts atomically.
  - Retry with same key returns original batch before stale-revision rejection,
    after current authorization.
  - Changed key/payload cannot reinsert those lines.
  - Max CSV 5 MiB/10000 rows; formula-looking text is not evaluated.
  - Escape spreadsheet-dangerous text in exported CSV; preserve original text/hash
    in the private JSON manifest rather than executing formulas in spreadsheets.


18.8 COMPLETED ROUTES AND ACCESS ABBREVIATIONS
------------------------------------------------
Access abbreviations used by every API block:
  U : Verified institution identity; no tenant access implied.
  O : ACTIVE OWNER in APPROVED institution.
  W : ACTIVE OWNER or MANAGER in APPROVED institution.
  R : Authorized read; W operational data, VIEWER released snapshots only.
  V : Independently provisioned scoped operator/reviewer; claimed case required.
  K : Separate named global registry/operator capability; never member role.
  - Two access labels on a combined route follow the method order shown there.
  - Current resource ancestry and feature availability are checked for every method.
  - Create/mutation routes require Idempotency-Key unless an explicit harmless
    preview/read exception is stated; registry draft creation also uses that key.
  - The new routes below close retrieval/edit/retry gaps in earlier summaries.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/campuses/{cid}/vegetation-assessments
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Read mask revisions and job/review state.

  Auth: R; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: Page query; optional boundary_id.
  Response: 200 Page<MaskResource>.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/campuses/{cid}/tree-surveys
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Find draft/resumable surveys without guessing IDs.

  Auth: W; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: Page query/status filter.
  Response: 200 Page<SurveyResource>.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Read stable identity/event history.

  Auth: R; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: Page query; VIEWER published snapshot lineage only.
  Response: 200 Page<TreeEventResource>; frozen event fields only.

  Auth: W; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: Page query; optional plot_id and since_input_revision.
  Response: 200 Page<MeasurementResource>.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Auth: W; same 15.4 access checks.

  Response: 200 LineMutationResult<MeasurementResource>.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Remove an unfrozen draft observation.

  Auth: W; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: No body; If-Match survey + Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 DeleteMutationResult; accepted -> 409.

  Auth: R; same 15.4 access checks.

  Response: 200 Page<AuditResource>.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Authorize report/map/template artifact download.

  Auth: R / V / K; same 15.4 access checks.

  Response: 200 DownloadResource; signed URL expiry per 21.1.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/jobs/{jid}/retry
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Resume an exact retriable failed job.

  Auth: W / original scoped operator; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: JobRetry + Idempotency-Key; unchanged input hash.
  Response: 202 same JobAccepted/job_id queued for its next bounded execution.

GET /api/v1/admin/review-cases
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Provide the required operator work queue.

  Auth: V; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: Page query; resource_type/status filters.
  Response: 200 Page<ReviewCaseResource> within assigned scope.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/admin/review-cases/{case_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Load exact frozen case and validation evidence.

  Auth: V; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: No body.
  Response: 200 ReviewCaseResource; scoped frozen data/evidence is in its declared fields.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/admin/review-cases/{case_id}/claim
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Claim a case for independent review.

  Auth: V; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: current_revision INTEGER; action CLAIM|RENEW|RELEASE
  DEFAULT CLAIM; Idempotency-Key. Only the claimant can renew/release.
  Response: 200 ReviewCaseResource; conflicting claim -> 409.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DELETE /api/v1/institutions/{iid}/invitations/{invite_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Revoke a pending invitation.

  Auth: O; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: No body; If-Match invitation + Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 revoked InvitationResource; accepted invite is not membership
  revoke.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET/PATCH /api/v1/institutions/{iid}/notifications
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Read own institution inbox and read flags.

  Auth: R / own pending creator; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: GET Page query; PATCH notification_id UUID,read=true.
  Response: 200 Page<NotificationResource> or updated own NotificationResource.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST/GET /api/v1/institution-account/deletion-requests
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Request/track institution-account deletion.

  Auth: U; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: POST DeletionCreate + Idempotency-Key; GET own open/latest request.
  Response: POST 201 / GET 200 DeletionResource; ownership action shown.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/institution-account/deletion-requests/{request_id}/cancel
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Cancel own unexecuted deletion request.

  Auth: U; same 15.4 access checks.
  Request: No body; If-Match request + Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 DeletionResource; PROCESSING/COMPLETED -> 409.

18.9 REGISTRY, OPERATOR AND OUTPUT CONTRACT COMPLETION
------------------------------------------------------
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SOURCE PROVENANCE: SourceMetadata
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Trace the reviewed bytes behind parameters and policies.

Fields:

  Field               | Type              | Required / meaning
  --------------------|-------------------|-------------------------------
  title               | TEXT              | Source title
  publisher           | TEXT              | Publishing organization
  source_version      | TEXT              | Exact source version
  source_url          | HTTPS URL or NULL | Original approved URL
  page                | TEXT or NULL      | Relevant page
  section             | TEXT or NULL      | Relevant section
  published_date      | DATE or NULL      | Source publication date
  retrieved_at        | timestamp         | Byte retrieval time
  evidence_id         | UUID              | READY operator-authorized source bytes
  checksum            | SHA-256           | Actual source byte checksum
  license             | TEXT              | Reviewed reuse conditions
  applicability_notes | TEXT              | Applicable geography/date/method limits

Validation:
  - Metadata alone does not prove review of the source content.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
REGISTRY INPUT: RegistryDraft
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Common metadata for FACTOR, METHOD, MODEL and KNOWLEDGE drafts.

Fields:

  Field               | Type              | Required / meaning
  --------------------|-------------------|-------------------------------
  kind                | TEXT              | FACTOR, METHOD, MODEL or KNOWLEDGE
  logical_key         | TEXT              | 1–120 characters
  version             | INTEGER           | >= 1
  schema_version      | INTEGER           | 1
  title               | TEXT              | 1–200 characters
  publisher           | TEXT              | Source publisher
  source_url          | HTTPS URL or NULL | Approved source location
  source_evidence_id  | UUID or NULL      | Source-byte evidence
  source_version      | TEXT              | Exact source version
  published_date      | DATE or NULL      | Source publication date
  license             | TEXT              | Reviewed license
  source_page         | TEXT or NULL      | Relevant page
  source_section      | TEXT or NULL      | Relevant section
  geography_codes     | TEXT[]            | Applicable locations
  valid_from          | DATE or NULL      | Start of applicability
  valid_to            | DATE or NULL      | End of applicability
  applicability_notes | TEXT              | Limits and assumptions
  config              | object            | The selected kind-specific config below

Validation:
  - Publication requires reviewed source evidence. A URL alone is not proof that
    source bytes were retrieved or reviewed.

REGISTRY CONFIG DISCRIMINATOR
------------------------------
RegistryConfig = FactorConfig | MethodConfig | ModelConfig | KnowledgeConfig.
RegistryDraft.kind selects exactly one config schema. Reject keys from another
kind, unknown keys and unknown schema versions.

DraftRegistryConfig:
  - DRAFT uses the selected config with recursively optional named object fields.
    Empty config={} is valid. Present values retain their declared scalar/array
    types, enum/range checks and explicit nullability; unknown keys are rejected.
  - Missing source/parameter/policy fields are draft blockers, never invented values.
    Nested object fragments are allowed; array items use the same named draft fields.
  - APPROVE/ACTIVATE compiles the COMPLETE selected RegistryConfig below, requiring
    every mandatory field, READY source bytes and independently reviewed validation.
  - Published config and parameter rows are immutable. Draft edit cannot change
    kind/logical_key/version, steal a child ID or overwrite another registry version.

Every complete-config field below is required unless marked optional. Nullable
fields still have keys. Scientific values remain reviewed commissioning inputs.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FACTOR CONFIG: FactorConfig
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field               | Type                         | Required / meaning
  --------------------|------------------------------|-------------------------------
  category            | TEXT                         | Supported method category
  subcategory         | TEXT                         | Exact activity schema value
  process             | TEXT                         | Exact applicable process
  fuel                | TEXT or NULL                 | Fuel when relevant
  material            | TEXT or NULL                 | Material when relevant
  value               | Decimal                      | >=0; exact sourced value
  numerator_unit      | TEXT                         | kgCO2e, tCO2e, kgCO2, kgCH4 or kgN2O
  denominator_unit    | TEXT                         | Exact EmissionLine.unit
  gas_basis           | TEXT                         | COMBINED_CO2E or GAS_SPECIFIC
  scope               | Scope                        | SCOPE_1, SCOPE_2 or SCOPE_3
  purpose             | TEXT                         | INVENTORY or AVOIDED; never interchangeable
  boundary            | TEXT                         | Source accounting boundary
  exclusions          | TEXT[]                       | Explicit exclusions
  mass_basis          | TEXT or NULL                 | WET or DRY where applicable
  standard_conditions | StandardConditions or NULL   | Source-defined gas-volume basis
  gwp                 | GwpConfig or NULL            | Required compatible basis for gas-specific conversion
  uncertainty         | ParameterUncertainty or NULL | Unknown remains NULL

Validation / behavior:
  - COMBINED_CO2E is already converted; never apply GWP again. No implicit density,
    volume, heating-value or efficiency conversion.
  - SourceMetadata and applicability dates/geography come from the registry metadata.
    Mapping to physical gas_basis/config is explicit in the factor serializer.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
STANDARD CONDITIONS: StandardConditions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field         | Type    | Required / meaning
  --------------|---------|-------------------------------
  temperature_k | Decimal | >0; absolute temperature
  pressure_kpa  | Decimal | >0; source pressure

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GWP CONFIG: GwpConfig
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field              | Type              | Required / meaning
  -------------------|-------------------|-------------------------------
  assessment_version | TEXT              | Exact reviewed assessment
  horizon_years      | INTEGER           | >0; exact assessment horizon
  gas_values         | map<TEXT,Decimal> | Exact gas keys CO2, CH4 and/or N2O supported by the source

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PARAMETER UNCERTAINTY: ParameterUncertainty
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field     | Type    | Required / meaning
  ----------|---------|-------------------------------
  lower     | Decimal | Lower bound
  upper     | Decimal | >=lower
  unit      | TEXT    | Same quantity unit as parameter
  basis     | TEXT    | Source-defined bounds or confidence procedure
  source_id | UUID    | Approved source/parameter reference

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
METHOD CONFIG: MethodConfig
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field               | Type                  | Required / meaning
  --------------------|-----------------------|-------------------------------
  parameters          | MethodParameterDraft[] | Explicit child parameter definitions; [] only if the method needs none
  formula_keys        | TEXT[]                | Supported implemented formulas in 17.1..17.5
  coverage_categories | TEXT[]                | Exact Declaration.category values
  included_scopes     | Scope[]               | Explicit inventory scope coverage
  pools               | TEXT[]                | Eligible carbon pools
  required_inputs     | JSON Schema           | Complete method-level submit requirements
  activity_schemas    | map<TEXT,JSON Schema> | Each supported activity/action in 18.11
  unit_conversions    | UnitConversion[]      | Only approved source-backed conversions
  factor_mappings     | FactorMapping[]       | Exact compatible approved factors
  quality_policy      | CampusQualityPolicy   | Complete sourced sampling/capture policy
  uncertainty_policy  | UncertaintyPolicy     | Supported propagation and unknown handling
  attribution_policy  | AttributionPolicy     | Evidence-based atmospheric assignment
  epsilon_tCO2e       | Decimal               | >=0; approved classification tolerance
  epsilon_rationale   | TEXT                  | Source/validation explanation for epsilon
  overlap_rules       | OverlapRule[]         | Deterministic prevention of double counting
  ranking_mode        | TEXT                  | RULES_V1 in the initial release
  ranking_config      | RankingConfig         | Versioned ordering in 20.1

Validation / behavior:
  - Schema dialect: JSON Schema Draft 2020-12. Root type=object,
    additionalProperties=false; recursively type every nested object/array. Decimal
    fields use string plus the 18.6 decimal pattern and explicit unit annotations.
  - activity_schemas keys are ELECTRICITY, TRANSPORT, STATIONARY_FUEL, WASTE, WATER,
    SOLAR, RAINWATER, BIOGAS and COMPOSTING when supported. A missing supported-key
    schema blocks its form; it does not fall back to an untyped object.
  - Compile schemas at publication and test formula reference cases independently.
    Server conditional/date/overlap/applicability checks remain authoritative.
  - Store quality/uncertainty/attribution child policies atomically with the method
    under 16.3; their IDs are server-assigned and available in the returned version.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
METHOD CHILD PARAMETER: MethodParameterDraft
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field           | Type                         | Presence | Meaning / validation
  ----------------|------------------------------|----------|-------------------------------
  id              | UUID                         | required | Caller-generated stable child UUID, validated within this draft method only
  logical_key     | TEXT                         | required | 1..120 characters; unique within method
  kind            | TEXT                         | required | Exact kind enum in 16.3
  value           | Decimal or NULL              | required | Sourced scalar; NULL only for a non-scalar equation kind
  unit            | TEXT                         | required | Exact source unit; never inferred
  formula_key     | TEXT or NULL                 | required | Implemented allowlisted formula, never executable source
  required_inputs | JSON Schema                  | required | Typed formula input specification
  source_metadata | SourceMetadata               | required | READY exact METHOD_SOURCE provenance at publication
  applicability   | JSON Schema                  | required | Reviewed domain/species/size/date restrictions
  uncertainty     | ParameterUncertainty or NULL | required | Sourced uncertainty, unknown remains NULL

Validation / behavior:
  - IDs permit references from quality policy, conversion and factor mappings in one
    draft without an undisclosed parameter-create API. Reject an ID belonging to
    another method, including another published version.
  - Materialize method_parameters from config.parameters under the same method
    transaction; methodology_id and actors are server-owned. Validate kinds,
    same-method references and independent source review before publication.
  - For a new published correction use new method/child UUIDs and explicit provenance,
    not an edit to historical parameters. Parameter content in the published config
    and child rows must match exactly.

  to         | TEXT    | Exact output unit

  source_id  | UUID    | Approved conversion parameter/source

CAMPUS QUALITY POLICY: CampusQualityPolicy
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field                        | Type               | Required / meaning
  -----------------------------|--------------------|-------------------------------
  measurement_protocol_version | UUID               | Exact approved measurement protocol content ID
  eligibility                  | QualityEligibility | Supported pools/species/allometry
  sampling                     | QualitySampling    | Reviewed reproducible stratum/plot design
  dimensions                   | QualityDimensions  | Exact valid DBH/height/protocol ranges
  gps                          | QualityGps         | Sourced accuracy/geometry tolerances
  evidence                     | QualityEvidence    | Required evidence and overrides
  species                      | QualitySpecies     | Confidence/unknown handling
  capture_flags                | TEXT[]             | Allowed flags including required honest missing-input flags
  temporal                     | QualityTemporal    | Observation/season/revisit compatibility
  validation                   | QualityValidation  | Actual support/holdout/validation conditions
  uncertainty                  | QualityUncertainty | Reviewed stock measurement uncertainty
  source_metadata              | SourceMetadata     | Exact reviewed source bytes

Validation / behavior:
  - These are the original policy groups in 17.8, expanded here into literal typed
    keys. Sampling/measurement protocols and numeric thresholds require independent
    review before publication.
  - The method child record supplies policy id/version; those storage fields are not
    extra keys in this config. Android reads this same object, especially
    measurement_protocol_version and gps.max_accuracy_m.
  - Every field is required unless explicitly nullable. Source-backed values are
    commissioned inputs, never farmer preview defaults or guessed minimum counts.

MODEL CONFIG: ModelConfig
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  -------------------------|----------------------|-------------------------------

Validation / behavior:
  - Artifact and validation bytes must be READY before activation. A metadata-only
    model cannot run campus inference.
  - No model training on a small current campus without the approved calibration plan.
    Ground and satellite estimate the same pool; reconcile rather than add their
    stocks.
