--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
KNOWLEDGE CONFIG: KnowledgeConfig
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fields:

  Field                    | Type              | Required / meaning
  -------------------------|-------------------|-------------------------------
  source_type              | TEXT              | URL or UPLOAD
  source_url               | HTTPS URL or NULL | Required for URL; checked against allowlist
  evidence_id              | UUID or NULL      | READY KNOWLEDGE evidence after upload
  topic_codes              | TEXT[]            | Topic enum in Section 20
  language                 | TEXT              | Explicit source language
  visibility               | TEXT              | PUBLIC_APPROVED
  supersedes_document_id   | UUID or NULL      | Existing document replaced by this version
  extraction_notes         | TEXT or NULL      | Extraction/table limitations
  source_byte_hash         | SHA-256 or NULL   | Required actual hash after retrieval
  ingestion_config_version | UUID              | Approved parser/chunking/index config

Validation / behavior:
  - Corpus/embedding/reranker versions are server-stored after ingestion. Create
    metadata DRAFT, obtain the document ID, upload KNOWLEDGE bytes, complete READY,
    reindex, independently review, then activate.
  - KNOWLEDGE uploads are global operator-only, institution_id=null. Never ingest
    tenant bills into the public corpus.
  - URL ingestion validates allowlisted HTTPS hosts on each redirect, rejecting
    local/private and DNS-rebinding destinations. Store actual retrieved
    bytes/hash/date and inspect licensing/extraction quality.
  - Activate only an independently reviewed SUCCEEDED candidate atomically. Retain the
    previous corpus until successful publication; reindex creates a new candidate,
    preserving old citations.

Reindex request:
  - Use ReindexRequest in 18.6. Hash is the current metadata/content snapshot.
  - An authorized retry with the same request resumes the same ingestion job.

Registry draft maintenance:
  GET /api/v1/admin/{registry}/{id} -> 200 RegistryResource.
  PATCH /api/v1/admin/{registry}/{id} -> 200 updated DRAFT RegistryResource.
  {registry} is exactly emission-factors, carbon-methodologies, models or
  knowledge-documents. Auth: K with matching scope; PATCH RegistryDraft changed
  fields + If-Match + Idempotency-Key. Required fields cannot be cleared.
  Author may edit its draft; independent reviewer may request correction.
  Published metadata/config is immutable: correction POST creates a new version.
  Dependent factor/method/model IDs never change after a frozen calculation.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/institutions/{iid}/invitations
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: List invitations independently of the members list.
  Auth: O.
  Request: Page query, status optional.
  Response: 200 Page<InvitationResource>; raw tokens/links are not listed.
  GET members returns Page<MembershipResource>; POST members returns invitation.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/admin/deletion-requests
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Privacy work queue.
  Auth: K PRIVACY_OPERATIONS.
  Request: Page query, status optional.
  Response: 200 Page<DeletionAdminResource>; privacy-operator scope only.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/admin/deletion-requests/{request_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Inspect verified deletion case and justified retention plan.
  Auth: K PRIVACY_OPERATIONS.
  Request: No body.
  Response: 200 DeletionAdminResource; private scoped retention/ownership plan.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/admin/deletion-requests/{request_id}/decision
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Record privacy decision/start scoped deletion worker.
  Auth: K PRIVACY_OPERATIONS; operator is distinct from requester.
  Request: DeletionDecision + If-Match + Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 DeletionResource for NEEDS_ACTION/REJECT; 202 JobAccepted for
  EXECUTE; requester polls its existing deletion-request route.
Deletion decision:
  - Use DeletionDecision, RetainedItem and PrivacyAction in 18.6.
  REJECT requires a documented reason, not an arbitrary refusal of account rights.
  EXECUTE requires verified recent request, no last-owner blocker and approved
  disclosed policy; idempotent job tracks provider/storage/Firebase cleanup.
  Status: REQUESTED -> NEEDS_ACTION -> REQUESTED on satisfied ownership action;
  REQUESTED -> APPROVED -> PROCESSING -> COMPLETED or FAILED_RETRYABLE;
  REQUESTED/NEEDS_ACTION -> CANCELLED or REJECTED with reason.
  FAILED_RETRYABLE retains the same plan and resumes through job retry.
  Personal notification read flags, settings and own privacy requests remain
  allowed for all verified institution users, including VIEWER/pending/revoked;
  they grant no access to tenant business resources. Feature-off does not remove
  own account/privacy/status access.

Review decision mapping:
  START claims an already assigned frozen case for UNDER_REVIEW.
  Registration uses APPROVE|RETURN|REJECT; boundary/mask/plot uses
  APPROVE|RETURN. Invalid/noneligible geometry remains NEEDS_CORRECTION with reason.
  Survey/tree-result review uses ACCEPT|RETURN; RETURN -> NEEDS_CORRECTION.
  Annual-audit review uses APPROVE|RETURN; APPROVE is a recorded decision
  at UNDER_REVIEW, followed by separate finalize. SUSPEND/RESUME are access actions
  requiring authority scope, reason and current revision; never accounting approval.
  Evidence/annotations attach to the reviewed hash; members cannot call review APIs.

Period and completeness:
  Initial annual conventions describe 12 calendar months in the selected timezone;
  CUSTOM shorter periods are explicitly labeled/disclosed and reviewed.
  Do not force 365 days across leap years. Finalization cannot precede period_end.
  Each included source declares full activity coverage or documented estimated
  coverage; missing months are not zero. Propose exclusions with evidence at submit;
  the reviewer accepts/rejects them. Positive contributions cannot use ZERO_CONFIRMED.
  Category draft subtotals remain possible; full actual_total NULL until complete
  for stated reviewed coverage. Zero denominators -> intensity NULL, not division.

Shared response types:
  Warning: code TEXT; message TEXT; path TEXT|null; severity INFO|WARNING|BLOCKING.
  TreeEventResource: exact fields in 18.6. Wire date maps to event_date;
  type maps to event_type. No second effective_date request/response key.
  AnalyticsQuery and AnalyticsResource: exact fields in 18.6.
  - Select up to 24 revisions over at most five years; no unbounded all-time read.
  - VIEWER receives released periods only; draft summary is OWNER/MANAGER-only.
  - Comparison requires the same stated metric/denominator and compatible periods.
    Prior zero/unknown or incompatibility returns NULL with comparison_reason.

Export content:
  PDF: institution/campus/period/revision, draft/synthetic badges, reviewed coverage,
  actual by category/scope, tree stock/removal/release, separate avoided/scenario
  results, balance status, uncertainties/exclusions and all method/source/hash IDs.
  CSV: separate typed sections/files for inputs, calculation lines, factors and
  totals; explicit column/units headers. Escape spreadsheet executable cells in
  labels/comments; preserve originals in JSON. Never put private bill bytes in CSV.
  JSON: schema_version=1, normalized snapshot + lineage/evidence IDs/checksums,
  formula terms/factors/results/quality and provenance; no credentials/signed URLs.
  Optional published advice refers to that exact immutable approved snapshot only.
  VIEWER downloads existing released exports; OWNER/MANAGER creates new exports.
  Evidence download has a separate authorization check. Private licensed source
  excerpts are bounded by license, not silently copied into public reports.
  Institution public pinning/anchoring is LATER and disabled; inventory/report
  completion has no Pinata/blockchain dependency or assumed missing pin endpoint.

Worker identity:
  Store actor user ID and scope at enqueue; use service credentials at execution.
  Recheck current actor/tenant access before publishing. Do not pass an expiring
  mobile token to a long-running job or treat ordinary app logout as account revoke.
  Real membership revocation/suspension/deletion blocks unauthorized publication.

18.10 CAMPUS CONFIGURATION AND BOUNDED INPUT CONTRACTS
-----------------------------------------------------
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/campuses/{cid}/assessment-config
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Resolve the approved measurement/satellite/offline configuration.
  Auth: W; institution/control/boundary scope must be valid.
  Request: Optional method_version/model_version/survey_id UUID; no body.
    survey_id resolves that owned DRAFT or ACCEPTED survey's exact approved
    lineage. DRAFT supports capture; ACCEPTED supports stock-job configuration.
  Response: 200 AssessmentConfig; missing approved measurement/sampling policy
    -> 503 METHOD_NOT_CONFIGURED. Model applicability is required when requesting
    a stock job; unavailable models do not prevent approved plot preparation.
Assessment configuration:
  - Use AssessmentConfig and its nested models in 18.6.
  - Published quality_policy keys are the complete CampusQualityPolicy in 18.9.
  - Model selection is limited to returned applicable versions and revalidated by
    the backend. No public registry-admin privilege or private provider data.

  Configuration stages:
    APPROVED boundary + mask + sampling policy, no plots:
      plot_set_id=NULL; enabled_actions includes GENERATE_PLOTS.
    APPROVED plots + measurement protocol:
      enabled_actions includes START_SURVEY; exact-survey grant follows creation.
    Accepted survey + applicable approved model:
      enabled_actions includes REQUEST_TREE_AUDIT.
    Missing model -> applicable_models=[] with explanatory requirements;
      REQUEST_TREE_AUDIT is disabled, without inventing a model version.

  Exact quality_policy keys consumed by Android:
    measurement_protocol_version UUID;
    gps {max_accuracy_m Decimal>0};
    all other coverage/range/uncertainty rules remain versioned server policy.
    No hardcoded institutional plot count, density, height or protocol fallback.

Input bounds and validation:
  - Request schema_version defaults 1; unknown versions fail clearly before writes.
  - Validate E.164 phone, IANA timezone, finite decimals and ISO dates; end>=start.
  - Units are exact catalog values; no locale/comma/unit inference.
  - Unspecified text default maximum 2000 characters; URL <=2048; source IDs <=120;
    names/tags have their smaller schema limits.
  - JSON nesting <=8 levels.
  - ID arrays <=200; form replacement <=200 lines/request/category.
  - Larger inputs use validated CSV and paging; no unbounded nested rows or queue
    payloads.
  - Methods may reduce limits for eligibility but cannot bypass request/file bounds.
  - Validate typed evidence/source/factor references and conditional
    baseline/project fields server-side; missing scientific source remains
    NEEDS_DATA/UNQUANTIFIED.
  - No undocumented numeric project_emissions override; required component rows and
    gas/mass/energy parameters follow the approved formula/input schema.
  - Decimal arithmetic preserves internal precision; wire quantization follows the
    approved rounding policy.
  - Threshold/classification uses unrounded values.
  - A missing config is an explicit release/runtime block, not an empty success.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/imports/{batch_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Resume preview/commit status and page normalized import rows.
  Auth: W; exact same audit/tenant and DRAFT or own operational history.
  Request: Page query; errors_only BOOLEAN optional; no body.
  Response: 200 ImportResource; exact row/count types are in 18.6.
  Cursor binds normalized hash/status; stale preview invalidates commit, not
  visibility of the explanation. Reopening a committed batch never recommits.

Typed baseline components:
  - other_components[] and baseline treatment/energy terms are {component_id,
    formula_key,quantity Decimal,unit,factor_id UUID,source_id,evidence_ids[],
    activity_start/end}; backend normalizes and calculates, never trusts totals.
  - Project terms reference actual same-period input rows.
  - Shared gas/stream/meter references forbid overlap with combined factors and
    other mitigation actions.
  - Typed config/conditional inputs require gas-specific fields, conversions and
    service boundaries.
  - Method required_inputs JSON Schema enforces exact names and allowed variants;
    unsupported extension cannot pass as arbitrary JSON.

Evidence hash binding and large-category editing:
  - Offline evidence references carry local evidence UUID/expected byte hash; server
    evidence ID mapping is acknowledged after upload/check READY.
  - Remap local references before MeasurementItem canonical hash/batch upload.
  - Exact hash algorithm/version is shared by queue and API adapter; local-only
    capture identity remains stable while the server payload gets its saved hash.
  - Draft category GET paging may exceed a 200-row replace request after CSV.
  - For those categories use bounded line mutations below, not a partial replace
    that drops unseen pages. replace=true still means FULL selected category.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATCH /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/emission-inputs/{line_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Edit one existing draft activity line in large/paged categories.
  Auth: W.
  Request: changed EmissionLine fields + If-Match audit + Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 LineMutationResult<EmissionLine>; category/scope cannot be changed
    through arbitrary payload. Validate source dates/factors/overlap and clear
    stale calculation pointer. Omitted fields remain unchanged.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DELETE /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/emission-inputs/{line_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Delete one unfrozen draft line with dependency validation.
  Auth: W.
  Request: No body; If-Match audit + Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 DeleteMutationResult; dependent included/mitigation refs
    -> 409 DEPENDENT_INPUTS_EXIST, resolve those first. Frozen history never deleted.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/emission-inputs
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Append one source line without replacing a paged category.
  Auth: W.
  Request: EmissionAppend + If-Match audit + Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 201 LineMutationResult<EmissionLine>; source overlap
    or reused line identity -> 409; referenced incompatible factor -> field error.
EmissionAppend:
  item EmissionLine; declaration Declaration for that same category.
  Reject replacement/deletion intent. Validate resulting category/coverage state;
  zero/not-applicable cannot hide the new positive line. Never edit frozen inputs.

================================================================================

18.11 COMPLETED WORKFLOWS AND EXACT SCHEMA RULES
------------------------------------------------
This section is part of the canonical API, not an alternative runtime design.
All mutation rules in 18/19.2 apply, including authorization-before-replay,
Idempotency-Key and quoted If-Match of the explicitly named resource.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/carbon-methodologies/{method_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Read an exact approved methodology version.
  Auth: OWNER/MANAGER with applicable method access, or matching operator capability.
  Request: Exact method_id. No body.
  Response: 200 RegistryResource.
  Validation:
    - Missing or incompatible version -> 404/422.
    - Deactivated versions remain readable through frozen historical snapshots.
      They cannot be selected for new work.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/campuses/{cid}/trees/{tid}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Read the selected tree without searching every inventory page.
  Auth: Authorized read in the matching campus/institution.
  Request: No body. VIEWER supplies the released annual snapshot_id.
  Response: 200 TreeResource.
  Validation:
    - OWNER/MANAGER can read the current operational identity.
    - VIEWER receives only the tree in its accessible finalized/superseded snapshot.
    - Wrong campus, institution or hidden snapshot -> protected 404.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/evidence
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: List evidence for one authorized parent.
  Auth: Own pending creator, OWNER/MANAGER, or matching operator capability.
  Request: parent_type, parent_id and Page query.
  Response: 200 Page<EvidenceResource>.
  Validation:
    - Include checked evidence links from correction revisions.
    - Do not enumerate another parent or institution. VIEWER has no private evidence access.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/mitigation-inputs
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Append one mitigation line without replacing unseen rows.
  Auth: ACTIVE OWNER/MANAGER in an APPROVED institution; audit is DRAFT.
  Request: {item: MitigationLine}; If-Match audit; Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 201 LineMutationResult<MitigationLine>.
  Validation: Check same-period actual-input references and overlap.
              Invalidate the previous preview pointer atomically.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATCH /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/mitigation-inputs/{line_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Edit one existing draft mitigation line.
  Auth: ACTIVE OWNER/MANAGER in an APPROVED institution; audit is DRAFT.
  Request: Changed MitigationLine fields; If-Match audit; Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 LineMutationResult<MitigationLine>.
  Validation: action/id are immutable. Revalidate actual-input references and overlap.
              Invalidate the previous preview pointer atomically.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DELETE /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/mitigation-inputs/{line_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Remove one unfrozen draft mitigation line.
  Auth: ACTIVE OWNER/MANAGER in an APPROVED institution; audit is DRAFT.
  Request: No body; If-Match audit; Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 DeleteMutationResult.
  Validation: Reject frozen/dependent rows. Invalidate the old preview atomically.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATCH /api/v1/institution-audits/{aid}/declarations/{category}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Purpose: Save a declaration without replacing a paginated category.
  Auth: ACTIVE OWNER/MANAGER in an APPROVED institution; audit is DRAFT.
  Request: Declaration for the exact path category; If-Match audit; Idempotency-Key.
  Response: 200 LineMutationResult<Declaration> (item is the declaration).
  Validation: Check ALL saved category rows, not just the displayed page.
              Invalidate the previous preview pointer atomically.


Campus control — no circular create/evidence/review dependency:
  1. POST campus with control_evidence_ids=[] is a valid PENDING draft.
  2. Upload evidence under CAMPUS parent=returned campus ID; complete/check READY.
  3. PATCH campus with complete control_evidence_ids freezes a CAMPUS_CONTROL case
     in review_cases; response CampusResource includes case ID and control_input_hash.
     Omitted evidence means no submission; empty list saves draft and cannot approve.
  4. Independent operator claims/inspects that exact control snapshot, then reviews.
  5. Boundary may be uploaded while control is PENDING. Geometry approval requires
     current control APPROVED and matching control_approved_hash.
  - Changing approved name/address/control evidence returns control to PENDING,
    blocks new fieldwork/jobs, and creates a new control snapshot/case.
  - Existing released history remains readable.
  - Do not mutate a claimed case snapshot.
  - Archived campus restore sets control PENDING; fresh control review is required.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/admin/campuses/{cid}/control-review
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Auth V with CAMPUS_CONTROL case; If-Match campus; ReviewRequest.
  START -> claimed review, APPROVE -> control APPROVED and saved approved hash;
  RETURN -> control PENDING + issues; REJECT -> control REJECTED + reason.
  200 CampusResource; approve only matching frozen evidence/hash and independent actor.
  Review decision also checks ReviewRequest.current_revision=case_input_revision.

Invitations — initial Android delivery uses explicit codes:
  - Generate a 32-byte cryptographically random token encoded base64url without
    padding.
  - Return invitation_token on create/reissue and authorized receipt replay only.
    Invitation rows store SHA-256; bounded encrypted receipt is defined in 19.2.
  - InvitationResource adds invitation_token TEXT|null (create/reissue only),
    invitation_url TEXT|null. Initial invitation_url=NULL. Lists omit both secrets.
  - Owner copies/shares the code through an explicit OS Share action.
  - Recipient selects Institution, signs in, opens Accept invitation in
    Setup/Profile/status, pastes the code and explicitly accepts.
  - Never treat a code as an account role.
  - Tokens are not notification routes, analytics fields, clipboard history owned by
    the app, or URLs.
  - Clear input after acceptance/cancel/sign-out.
  - Automatic token links and production HTTPS App Links are LATER; no unconfigured
    website, host association or hidden login deep-link behavior is required.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/institutions/{iid}/invitations/{invite_id}/reissue
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Auth: ACTIVE OWNER in APPROVED institution.
  If-Match invitation; no body. 201 NEW InvitationResource/new token.
  In one transaction revoke old pending invitation and create its successor with
  same target/permission, new 7-day expiry and supersedes_id. Accepted -> 409.
  Replayed same key returns original created secret to same authorized caller only;
  encrypt the bounded idempotency receipt at rest; do not log the token.

Controlled additional-tenant creation:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
POST /api/v1/admin/institution-registration-authorizations
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Auth K REGISTRATION_OPERATIONS; body {target_user_id UUID,reason TEXT(1..2000)}.
  Target must be an existing approved institution OWNER. 201 {id,target_user_id,
  expires_at,consumed_at:null}; expires after 7 days; no bearer secret.
  InstitutionCreate.operator_authorization_id references this row. Lock/consume
  it transactionally with tenant creation; check target/current owner/expiry.
  Expose usable IDs only to that target via /auth/me.registration_authorizations[].
  Expired/used/wrong-target grant -> 409 REGISTRATION_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID.
  First restricted identity needs no grant. Existing users join other tenants
  through invitations; a client-supplied institution ID is never an authorization.

Personal inbox without membership:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET /api/v1/institution-account/notifications
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Auth: Verified institution identity; no tenant authority.
  Page query. 200 Page<NotificationResource> for current user only.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATCH /api/v1/institution-account/notifications/{notification_id}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Auth: Verified institution identity; no tenant authority.
  body NotificationPatch; If-Match notification. 200 NotificationResource.
  These are canonical personal inbox routes. No selected institution is required.
  The tenant notification GET route is a filtered view of the same own inbox;
  its PATCH accepts own notification_id and has identical authorization. No broadcasts.
  Pending/revoked users see safe status summaries; opening a record reauthorizes it.

Resource/snapshot visibility:
  - R endpoints for campus/profile/tree/boundary/mask/plots accept snapshot_id.
  - For VIEWER it is required except list-campus/list-assessment discovery, which
    derives only released linked summaries.
  - Resolve the selected annual snapshot, verify same institution and resource
    inclusion, and serialize its historical fields.
  - No snapshot -> 422 SNAPSHOT_REQUIRED; hidden snapshot/resource -> 404.
  - W may omit snapshot_id for current operational data.
  - Archived campuses remain readable through released snapshots but cannot start
    new work.
  - Direct tree-survey/measurement/raw evidence routes remain W/V as specified.
  - VIEWER inventory uses only the tree identity/measurement summaries embedded in
    the accepted tree-assessment snapshot; never a current-tree join.
  - GET evidence/download and artifact/download allow V only for the claimed case
    and frozen evidence/artifacts being reviewed; reviewer is neither W nor member.

ACTIVITY DETAIL FIELDS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Define the exact nested fields used by the activity and mitigation forms.

Reading rules:
  - Tables use object.field paths, such as baseline.energy_factor_id.
  - Every object rejects unknown fields (additionalProperties=false).
  - A nullable optional field may be omitted or explicitly NULL.
  - Decimal, date and UUID rules are the same as in 18.6.
  - EvidenceRefs means UUID[] of authorized READY evidence.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: Component
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of Component.

Fields:

  Field          | Type              | Required / meaning
  ---------------|-------------------|-------------------------------
  component_id   | UUID              | Stable source component
  formula_key    | TEXT              | Approved calculation formula
  quantity       | Decimal           | Source quantity, not an emissions override
  unit           | EmissionLine.unit | Unit matching the formula/factor
  factor_id      | UUID              | Approved factor
  source_id      | TEXT              | Meter/asset/invoice source
  evidence_ids   | UUID[]            | READY evidence references
  activity_start | DATE              | Inclusive start
  activity_end   | DATE              | Inclusive end

Validation:
  - The backend calculates each term. Clients cannot supply an unexplained emissions
    total.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: StandardConditions
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of StandardConditions.

Fields:

  Field         | Type    | Required / meaning
  --------------|---------|-------------------------------
  temperature_k | Decimal | Required; > 0
  pressure_kpa  | Decimal | Required; > 0


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: StockReconciliation
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of StockReconciliation.

Fields:

  Field              | Type    | Required / meaning
  -------------------|---------|-------------------------------
  opening_quantity   | Decimal | Required; >= 0; same unit as line
  purchased_quantity | Decimal | Required; >= 0; same unit as line
  closing_quantity   | Decimal | Required; >= 0; same unit as line
  other_in_quantity  | Decimal | Required; >= 0; same unit as line
  other_out_quantity | Decimal | Required; >= 0; same unit as line
  evidence_ids       | UUID[]  | Supporting stock/consumption evidence

Validation:
  - Consumption = opening + purchased + other_in - closing - other_out. Purchases
    alone are not consumption.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: ELECTRICITY details
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of ELECTRICITY details.

Fields:

  Field                 | Type            | Required / meaning
  ----------------------|-----------------|-------------------------------
  grid_code             | TEXT            | Required
  geography_code        | TEXT            | Required
  meter_id              | TEXT            | Required
  included_submeter_ids | TEXT[]          | Known included submeters
  total_load_kWh        | Decimal or NULL | Optional total-load reading

Validation:
  - quantity uses kWh. The selected factor purpose is INVENTORY. Solar is recorded
    separately, without a second deduction.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: TRANSPORT details
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of TRANSPORT details.

Fields:

  Field                | Type                        | Required / meaning
  ---------------------|-----------------------------|-------------------------------
  fuel                 | TEXT                        | PETROL, DIESEL or CNG
  vehicle_class        | TEXT                        | Required classification
  consumption_basis    | TEXT                        | METERED, LOG_RECONCILED or ESTIMATED
  stock_reconciliation | StockReconciliation or NULL | Optional; supporting consumption calculation
  standard_conditions  | StandardConditions or NULL  | Required for std_m3
  density_source_id    | UUID or NULL                | Required for cross-mass/volume conversion

Validation:
  - ESTIMATED consumption requires estimate_notes. Match gas standard conditions and
    density sources to the selected factor.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: STATIONARY_FUEL details
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of STATIONARY_FUEL details.

Fields:

  Field                         | Type                        | Required / meaning
  ------------------------------|-----------------------------|-------------------------------
  fuel                          | TEXT                        | LPG, PETROL, DIESEL or CNG
  purpose                       | TEXT                        | KITCHEN, GENERATOR, BOILER or OTHER
  consumption_basis             | TEXT                        | METERED, LOG_RECONCILED or ESTIMATED
  stock_reconciliation          | StockReconciliation or NULL | Optional supporting consumption
  standard_conditions           | StandardConditions or NULL  | Required for std_m3
  density_source_id             | UUID or NULL                | Required for cross-mass/volume conversion
  consumed_cylinder_equivalents | Decimal or NULL             | Required with cylinder-based LPG input
  verified_net_kg_per_cylinder  | Decimal or NULL             | Required with cylinder equivalents
  gas_components                | GasComponent[] or NULL      | When selected method uses separate gases

Validation:
  - Provide both cylinder fields or neither. Derive LPG quantity in kg and reject a
    conflicting manually supplied quantity.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: WASTE details
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of WASTE details.

Fields:

  Field                        | Type                   | Required / meaning
  -----------------------------|------------------------|-------------------------------
  material_code                | TEXT                   | Required
  pathway                      | TEXT                   | LANDFILL, RECYCLING, COMPOSTING, DIGESTION, INCINERATION or OTHER
  treatment_operator           | TEXT                   | Required
  treatment_control            | TEXT                   | ONSITE_CONTROLLED or OUTSOURCED
  mass_basis                   | TEXT                   | WET or DRY
  feedstock_stream_id          | TEXT                   | Stable stream identity
  included_energy_input_ids    | UUID[]                 | Already-included energy components
  included_transport_input_ids | UUID[]                 | Already-included transport components
  gas_components               | GasComponent[] or NULL | When selected method uses separate gases

Validation:
  - ONSITE_CONTROLLED process gases are Scope 1; OUTSOURCED treatment uses the
    selected covered Scope 3 method.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: WATER details
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of WATER details.

Fields:

  Field                      | Type            | Required / meaning
  ---------------------------|-----------------|-------------------------------
  process                    | TEXT            | PUMPING, SUPPLY or WASTEWATER
  energy_source              | TEXT            | GRID, ONSITE or SUPPLIER_PROCESS
  meter_id                   | TEXT or NULL    | Pumping meter if applicable
  supplier_boundary          | TEXT or NULL    | Supplier/process coverage
  utilized_water_kL          | Decimal or NULL | For approved volume-based pumping estimate
  energy_intensity_source_id | UUID or NULL    | Reviewed intensity for the estimate

Validation:
  - PUMPING quantity uses kWh. A volume estimate requires utilized water and a
    reviewed intensity. SUPPLY/WASTEWATER uses m3 or kL.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DETAIL MODEL: GasComponent
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of GasComponent.

Fields:

  Field                       | Type                      | Required / meaning
  ----------------------------|---------------------------|-------------------------------
  gas                         | TEXT                      | CO2_FOSSIL, CH4, N2O or CO2_BIOGENIC
  stream_id                   | TEXT                      | Required; overlap identity
  route                       | TEXT                      | MEASURED_MASS or ACTIVITY_FACTOR
  mass_kg                     | Decimal or NULL           | Required for MEASURED_MASS
  activity_quantity           | Decimal or NULL           | Required for ACTIVITY_FACTOR
  activity_unit               | EmissionLine.unit or NULL | Required for ACTIVITY_FACTOR
  gas_factor_id               | UUID or NULL              | Required for ACTIVITY_FACTOR
  gwp_source_id               | UUID or NULL              | Approved GWP required for CH4/N2O
  included_combined_factor_id | UUID or NULL              | Combined-factor overlap reference

Validation:
  - MEASURED_MASS requires mass_kg. ACTIVITY_FACTOR requires activity quantity, unit
    and gas factor.
  - CH4/N2O require an approved GWP. Never count a gas again when its combined
    factor already includes it.
  - Draft quantities may be missing; submit requires the selected method’s complete
    declared gas coverage.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
MITIGATION INPUT: SOLAR
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of MitigationLine.baseline, project and attributes.

Fields:

  Field                               | Type            | Required / meaning
  ------------------------------------|-----------------|-------------------------------
  project.generated_kWh               | Decimal or NULL | Nonnegative measured input
  project.self_used_kWh               | Decimal or NULL | Nonnegative measured input
  project.exported_kWh                | Decimal or NULL | Nonnegative measured input
  project.losses_kWh                  | Decimal or NULL | Nonnegative measured input
  project.meter_refs                  | TEXT[]          | Supporting meter references
  project.project_component_input_ids | UUID[]          | Same-period actual project components
  baseline.displaced_self_kWh         | Decimal or NULL | Eligible displaced self-use
  baseline.eligible_export_kWh        | Decimal or NULL | Separately assessed eligible export
  baseline.displaced_grid_factor_id   | UUID or NULL    | Approved avoided-grid factor
  baseline.export_factor_id           | UUID or NULL    | Separate export assessment factor
  attributes.ownership                | TEXT            | OWNED, LEASED, THIRD_PARTY or UNKNOWN
  attributes.transferred_attributes   | BOOLEAN or NULL | Known renewable-attribute transfer
  attributes.assessment_boundary      | TEXT            | Declared project boundary


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
MITIGATION INPUT: RAINWATER
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of MitigationLine.baseline, project and attributes.

Fields:

  Field                               | Type            | Required / meaning
  ------------------------------------|-----------------|-------------------------------
  project.utilized_kL                 | Decimal or NULL | Actually used rainwater
  project.pumping_treatment_input_ids | UUID[]          | Actual energy/treatment components
  project.other_component_input_ids   | UUID[]          | Other actual components
  baseline.source                     | TEXT            | Baseline service source
  baseline.route                      | TEXT            | ENERGY or PROCESS_FACTOR
  baseline.energy_intensity_source_id | UUID or NULL    | Approved baseline intensity
  baseline.energy_factor_id           | UUID or NULL    | Required for ENERGY route
  baseline.process_factor_id          | UUID or NULL    | Required for PROCESS_FACTOR route
  baseline.other_components           | Component[]     | Other supported baseline terms
  attributes.assessment_boundary      | TEXT            | Declared project boundary

Validation:
  - ENERGY derives intensity from the approved source ID. Do not accept a
    conflicting scalar override.
  - PROCESS_FACTOR uses utilized volume and a matching service factor. Do not add
    energy already covered by that factor.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
MITIGATION INPUT: BIOGAS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of MitigationLine.baseline, project and attributes.

Fields:

  Field                               | Type               | Required / meaning
  ------------------------------------|--------------------|-------------------------------
  project.produced_std_m3             | Decimal or NULL    | Gas produced at stated conditions
  project.used_std_m3                 | Decimal or NULL    | Gas actually utilized
  project.standard_conditions         | StandardConditions | Required
  project.methane_fraction            | Decimal or NULL    | Composition in [0,1]
  project.LHV_source_id               | UUID or NULL       | Approved biogas heating value
  project.route                       | TEXT               | HEAT or ELECTRICITY
  project.efficiency_source_id        | UUID or NULL       | Approved useful-output efficiency
  project.useful_output               | object or NULL     | value Decimal, unit MJ/kWh, source TEXT, evidence_ids UUID[]
  project.project_component_input_ids | UUID[]             | Actual digestion/combustion/leakage/energy components
  project.feedstock_input_ids         | UUID[]             | Actual feedstock identity
  baseline.displaced_fuel             | TEXT               | LPG, PETROL, DIESEL, CNG or GRID
  baseline.heating_value_source_id    | UUID or NULL       | Displaced-fuel heating value
  baseline.efficiency_source_id       | UUID or NULL       | Baseline-device efficiency
  baseline.energy_factor_id           | UUID or NULL       | Approved displaced-energy factor
  baseline.treatment_components       | Component[]        | Supported baseline treatment terms
  baseline.selected_boundary          | TEXT               | Declared included services
  attributes.assessment_boundary      | TEXT               | Declared project boundary

Validation:
  - 0 <= used_std_m3 <= produced_std_m3. Methane fraction is in [0,1].
  - Measured and calculated useful output describe the same service; contribute once
    even when both are recorded.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Fields of MitigationLine.baseline, project and attributes.

Fields:

  Field                              | Type         | Required / meaning
  -----------------------------------|--------------|-------------------------------
  project.feedstock_input_ids        | UUID[]       | Actual feedstock identity
  project.mass_basis                 | TEXT         | WET or DRY
  project.actual_treatment_input_ids | UUID[]       | Actual composting inventory components
  project.other_component_input_ids  | UUID[]       | Other actual project components
  baseline.disposal_pathway          | TEXT         | Supported baseline disposal route
  baseline.disposal_factor_id        | UUID or NULL | Approved disposal factor
  baseline.other_components          | Component[]  | Other supported baseline terms
  baseline.evidence_ids              | UUID[]       | READY baseline evidence
  attributes.assessment_boundary     | TEXT         | Declared project boundary


COMMON MITIGATION VALIDATION
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - All arrays contain at most 200 items.
  - Quantities are nonnegative. NULL means an incomplete draft, not zero.
  - Missing required source, energy or fate data prevents quantification.
  - An empty project-component list does not prove zero project emissions.
    The approved method and READY evidence must establish absence/exclusion.
  - Method activity_schemas must implement these exact supported variants.
    An unsupported extension requires a new reviewed schema/method version.

Allocation versus submeter inclusion:
  - included_in_input_id always references one parent EmissionLine in the SAME
    audit, with same covered source/date boundary and no cycle.
  - allocation_ratio nonnull -> allocation display child; quantities/factors are
    derived from parent share and do not contribute again.
  - Full declared allocation must sum to 1 (exact Decimal); partial allocation
    states its unallocated share.
  - allocation_ratio NULL with included parent -> measured submeter attribution.
  - Validate compatible energy/process coverage; extra contribution is zero.
  - No included parent -> potential contributing source.
  - Deduplicate source_id, category/process and overlapping dates under audit-row
    lock.
  - Parent references and shared stream IDs cannot cross institutions/periods or
    form cycles.

Factor resolution and period rules:
  - Select only published approved factor IDs permitted by the selected method,
    matching process/fuel, purpose INVENTORY|AVOIDED, geography, unit/mass/gas basis
    and the ENTIRE activity date range. Preserve an explicit valid selected ID.
  - Otherwise choose unique most-specific geography match from method mappings;
    multiple valid candidates -> 422 FACTOR_SELECTION_REQUIRED.
  - Never use floating latest version.
  - Validity change inside activity dates -> split the activity.
  - Factor config adds purpose INVENTORY|AVOIDED; freeze source/GWP snapshots.
  - Corrections keep the period/timezone/campus boundary snapshot unless an explicit
    reviewed revision proposes a change.
  - Period family dates become immutable at first submission.
  - Later corrections cannot move history into another family.
  - Current-period drafts may include known activity through today.
  - Future activity, measured_at after server time, or submit before period_end ->
    422 PERIOD_NOT_ENDED (timestamps allow maximum 5-minute device skew; receipt
    time is server-owned).
  - Unsupported attribution/uncertainty may leave tree balance NULL without blocking
    an honestly disclosed, otherwise complete emissions-only report.

Result units and server-owned derivations:
  - ResultResource actual_total/quantified_actual_subtotal/by_scope/by_category,
    avoided amounts and inventory_balance/scenario_balance use tCO2e.
  - tree_stock is standing stock_tCO2, removal/release use tCO2 for this initial
    woody-carbon pool. tree_stock_tC additionally exposes tonnes C.
  - The CO2 gas conversion to CO2e is 1:1; never label standing stock as annual
    removal.
  - ResultResource adds tree_coverage {stock_complete BOOLEAN,change_complete BOOLEAN,
    missing_campus_ids UUID[],stock_subtotal_tCO2 Decimal|null}. Unknown whole-campus
    coverage keeps tree_stock/removal/release NULL while labeled known subtotals
    may display. A verified absence of included trees needs explicit reviewed
    boundary evidence to yield zero; missing tree links never imply zero.
  - Source metadata in a request is a proposal/evidence reference, never approval.
  - Root write schemas optionally accept schema_version=1; unknown version ->422.
  - MeasurementItem has only its listed keys (no local/schema metadata additions).
  - New emission/mitigation line IDs may be client-generated UUIDs for draft
    identity; validate ownership/collision before insert.
  - They never select tenant.
  - Unversioned campus control cases use case.revision=1 plus the frozen
    resource_input_revision/hash; business-revisioned registrations/surveys/audits
    use their actual revision.
  - Each case has separate case_input_revision.
  - Institution creation binds READY pretenant evidence and returns BOTH
    institution_id and registration_id.
  - InstitutionResource includes current registration input_revision separately when
    editing own pending registration; If-Match pending PATCH targets that
    registration, approved profile PATCH targets institution.
  - Submit/revisions likewise target registration, not profile counter.

Evidence, exports and wire recovery:
  - GeoJSON boundary uploads are JSON request bodies (<=1 MiB), not evidence MIME.
  - Evidence MIME allowlist is PDF/JPEG/PNG and audit-parent CSV.
  - MODEL-only uploads may use application/octet-stream for reviewed
    .json/.ubj/.tflite artifacts, max 20 MiB, private quarantine and format/hash
    checks; no pickle/code execution.
  - GEE models use a checked JSON descriptor of immutable asset/schema/hash instead.
  - Expired upload URL: repeat UploadCreate with NEW transport key and same existing
    evidence_id (optional UUID field).
  - Server validates owner/parent/hash/size, renews that PENDING_UPLOAD/UPLOADED
    object URL, returns same ID; max one renewal per attempt.
  - READY/rejected object bytes cannot be overwritten.
  - Renew is not a new evidence link.
  - UploadComplete acknowledgment is saved before polling; resource_id identifies
    evidence.
  - Only matching READY bytes/parent may enter measurements or submit hash.
  - CSV export is one UTF-8 RFC4180 table: section,record_id,parent_id,field,value,unit.
    section=INPUT|CALCULATION|FACTOR|TOTAL|PROVENANCE; value is escaped text or
    canonical JSON for structured fields. No ZIP/multi-file convention is implicit.
  - ExportResource exposes template_version, advice IDs/hashes and artifact_id;
    alias download_artifact_id maps to artifact_id. include_advice captures exact
    PUBLISHED final-context advice IDs/hashes at enqueue.
  - Publishing/withdrawal before download rechecks visibility; no silently changed
    report contents.
  - Malformed JSON/header -> 400; missing required If-Match -> 428;
    missing/invalid authentication -> 401; typed schema violation -> 422;
    lifecycle/revision/duplicate conflict -> 409; authorization errors remain
    401/403/404.
  - METHOD_NOT_CONFIGURED -> 503 consistently for enabled-but-unconfigured actions.
  - Required precondition missing -> 428; no alternate unquoted ETag convention.

================================================================================

SECTION 19: v4.0 — SERVICES, JOBS, LIFECYCLE AND EXPORTS
================================================================================

19.0 IMPLEMENTATION FILE MAP
---------------------------
Purpose: Name every institution module and its implementation responsibility.
Paths are relative to backend/. This is the required layout for a fresh build.
An existing equivalent module may use a thin adapter at this boundary.
Farmer modules retain their baseline paths, routes and persistence behavior.

BACKEND FILE TREE
-----------------
backend/
|-- main.py                              Existing FastAPI entry; mount routers below
|-- app/
|   |-- __init__.py
|   |-- config.py                        Existing loader; institution keys in 21.6
|   |-- dependencies.py                  Existing Firebase identity and DB connection
|   |-- institution_access.py            Verified actor, tenant ancestry, R/W/V guards
|   |-- institution_errors.py            Error/status translation; JSON-pointer fields
|   `-- institution_context.py           Request ID, transaction and generation scope
|-- routers/
|   |-- __init__.py
|   |-- institutions.py                  Profile, registration, invitations, members
|   |-- campuses.py                      Control, boundary, mask, plots, config
|   |-- campus_trees.py                  Tree/event, survey, measurements, stock jobs
|   |-- institution_audits.py            Periods, lines, declarations, imports, results
|   |-- institution_reports.py           Trends, exports, artifact authorization
|   |-- institution_evidence.py          Create/complete/list/private download
|   |-- institution_account.py           Personal inbox, privacy/deletion
|   |-- institution_admin.py             Scoped independent claims/decisions/finalize
|   |-- institution_registries.py        Registry author/review/activation and reads
|   |-- advisory.py                      Ask/history/source/publish/scenarios
|   `-- institution_jobs.py              Authorized job status/retry
|-- models/institution/
|   |-- __init__.py
|   |-- common.py                        Success, Error, Page, metadata, Decimal types
|   |-- auth.py                          Domain selection, flat bootstrap, membership
|   |-- institutions.py                  Profile, registration, grants, invitations
|   |-- campuses.py                      Campus/control/geometry/mask/plot/config
|   |-- trees.py                         Tree/events, measurements, quality, receipts
|   |-- surveys.py                       Survey, progress, stock assessment
|   |-- accounting.py                    Period, lines, declarations, results, exports
