22.2 ORDERED MIGRATIONS AND CLEAN BUILD CHECKLIST
------------------------------------------------
  SQL files below implement the physical dictionary in 16.0 and constraints in 16.1.
  Runner: scripts/institution/migrate.py. Run from backend/ using the baseline
  Python environment and DB credentials in 21.6. No database URL in CLI/log output.
  Command: python -m scripts.institution.migrate --check
  Command: python -m scripts.institution.migrate --apply
  --check is read-only and reports pending/checksum-mismatched versions.
  --apply acquires advisory lock hashtext('terratrust:institution:migrations').
  Create the migration ledger once before the first additive step:
    institution_schema_migrations
      version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY
      filename TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
      sha256 CHAR(64) NOT NULL
      applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
  This operational ledger is separate from business resources in 16.0.
  Hash raw UTF-8 SQL bytes; record the row in the same transaction as its step.
  Apply pending versions in numeric order; mismatch fails without executing SQL.
  A failed step rolls back. Never edit an applied migration or rerun raw seeds.
  --check/--apply exit 0 on success and nonzero on lock/schema/checksum failure.
  Read trusted deployment env/flags from 21.6, never a CLI production override.
  Production skips 010 and refuses any demo grants/registry restore configuration.
  001_extensions.sql: retain pgcrypto/PostGIS; enable btree_gist for UUID/range
    exclusion. Enable vector only when advisor storage is commissioned.
  002_user_domain.sql: add domain/onboarding fields, backfill existing nonstaff
    users FARMER; preserve inspected staff roles; add domain-specific nullable
    constraints without weakening existing FARMER validation or altering its rows.
  003_registries.sql: factors/methods/parameters/quality/models/knowledge/corpus
    and controlled deployment policies (retention/provider/retrieval/ranking).
  004_tenants.sql: institutions, registrations, memberships, invitations,
    additional-registration grants. Add circular current pointers after both tables.
  005_campus.sql: campus/control, boundaries, masks, plot sets/plots, trees/events,
    surveys/measurements/stock audits. Add current/latest pointers last.
  006_accounting.sql: periods/revisions/inputs/links/import batches+rows,
    immutable calculation snapshots+lines/final results/exports/artifacts.
  007_evidence_advisory.sql: evidence and reference links; advisory query/retrieval/
    recommendations/scenarios/feedback; indexes per corpus dimension.
  008_operations.sql: jobs/outbox/events/idempotency/review cases/grants,
    notifications/deletion requests; complete deferred circular FKs.
  009_constraints_access.sql: tenant+parent composite FKs, range/partial unique
    indexes, immutable-history/last-owner/parent-check triggers and private access.
  010_demo_test_only.sql: development registry/session tables and restore grants;
    production migration runner skips this step and rejects any installed reset
    grants/configuration. It still continues to applicable production step 011.
  011_institution_production_contract.sql: source_page/source_section fields,
    raw_input/derived_dbh_cm and GPS nullability; receipt generation/tombstone/local AES encryption
    columns; evidence/artifact purge fields; working-revision uniqueness and vector
    dimension checks/index definitions in this review. See preflight in 21.7.

Required SQL patterns (apply to each corresponding table, not just the example):
  CREATE UNIQUE INDEX campuses_tenant_id ON campuses(institution_id,id);
  ALTER TABLE campus_boundaries ADD CONSTRAINT boundary_campus_scope
    FOREIGN KEY(institution_id,campus_id) REFERENCES campuses(institution_id,id)
    ON DELETE RESTRICT;
  CREATE UNIQUE INDEX audit_one_working ON institution_audits(logical_audit_id)
    WHERE business_status IN ('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','UNDER_REVIEW');
  CREATE UNIQUE INDEX audit_one_final ON institution_audits(logical_audit_id)
    WHERE business_status='FINALIZED';
  - Use DEFERRABLE foreign keys for current pointers verified at commit.
  - Child ownership/family matching is checked by constraints plus transaction
    validation.
  - Row lock tenant for last-owner/invitation/grant/period overlap, family for
    correction/finalization, resource for draft writes, case for claims/decisions.
  - Lock order tenant -> family/campus -> resource -> case/job avoids deadlocks.
  - Immutable-row triggers reject business-field update/delete on frozen resources,
    accepted surveys/assessments, finalized/superseded audits, calculation
    snapshots, published registries and READY evidence bytes.
  - Narrow service paths may update lifecycle pointers/status/review metadata; log
    event in the same transaction.
  - Draft delete cannot cascade into accepted history.
  - Correction evidence uses institution_evidence_links.
  - Enforce allowed same-family ancestry in triggers.
  - LIVE accepted output cannot depend on SYNTHETIC scientific registry/artifact
    inputs.
  - Nonproduction test cases retain their explicit origin/quality badge.
  - New tables deny direct anon/authenticated client grants; API DB role is scoped
    service identity and all endpoints enforce Firebase-derived permissions.
  - RLS, if used, receives transaction-local verified scope; never
    Firebase=auth.uid().
  - Index tenant/status/date lists, GiST all spatial columns, GIN lexical vectors,
    job status/lease/outbox age, review scope/status, unique UUID/idempotency keys.

From-scratch sequence using these five files:
  1. Use farmer prerequisites/structure as deployed baseline; keep its behavior.
  2. Apply additive migrations with feature flags false; verify schema/constraints.
  3. Create private buckets; load keys/config in 21.6; start API/outbox/watchdog/
     separate workers. Provision least-privilege operator grants out of band.
  4. Enter independently sourced methods/factors/quality/model/corpus via registry
     APIs. Separate authors/reviewers; publish/activate validated versions only.
  5. Implement FDD 22.3 modules, FFC 12.5/12.6 routes, and shared-auth domain dispatch.
  6. Exercise setup -> approved campus -> survey -> accepted stock -> accounting
     -> review/finalize -> report/advice, then corrections/offline/access failures.
  7. Provision Demo 18 checkpoints and verify every manifest/reset assertion.
  8. Record SRS 35/36 evidence; enable restricted institution pilot, then advisor.
  Rollback: disable new work/pause new queues and revert compatible application
  deployment. Retain additive schema and accepted records; no down-migration wipe.

================================================================================
SECTION 23: IMPLEMENTED BACKEND CONTRACT RESOLUTIONS

Implementation review date: 2026-10-21 (Asia/Kolkata).
Scope: additive institution backend in this repository. Farmer business services,
farmer task application, wallets, minting and demo accounts 1-4 are preserved.
This addendum supersedes conflicting implementation/configuration details below;
it does not replace the scientific evidence and release gates in SRS 35-36.
No production database connection, migration, deployment, real provider request,
or real checkpoint restore was executed for this implementation review.
The ordered operator handoff is INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md in the repository root.

23.1 CONFIGURATION, ISOLATION AND MIGRATIONS
- The source is now present. Historical documentation-only review statements
  describe the earlier review, not the current implementation or live readiness.
- Add INSTITUTION_SCHEMA_ENABLED, default false. Use migrations/institution phases
  001-011 only through scripts.institution.migrate --offline/--check/--apply.
  --offline never imports credentials; --check is read-only; --apply alone writes.
  Phase 009 has two ordered files; the ledger checksum covers filenames and bytes.
- New institution tasks use tasks.institution_app:institution_app with isolated
  queues. This deliberately supersedes the same-Celery-app recommendation in 19.0:
  the production farmer Celery application is unchanged. Run a separate beat and
  institution workers; do not route their jobs to farmer workers.
- app.institution_database owns a lazy, certificate-verifying API SQL pool. Worker
  loops use separate verified NullPool engines. No institution startup migration.
- Phase 011 resolves physical-card omissions: nullable stock_tC and signed/gain/
  loss/removal/release_tCO2 on campus_tree_audits; measurements gain server-owned
  last_survey_input_revision; identity unlinking gains institution_deleted_uid_hash.
  Null UID/phone is allowed only for deleted INSTITUTION rows; FARMER identities
  continue to require both. PostgreSQL lowercases unquoted mixed-case physical
  names; the repository maps them back to the documented wire names.
- institution_provider_operations is an internal phase-011 table, not a mobile
  resource: UUID id/job_id, SHA-256 request_hash, operation, pinned model, status
  STARTED|SUCCEEDED|REJECTED|UNKNOWN, response/error_code, timestamps; unique
  (job_id,request_hash). It stores bounded provider responses/checkpoints and
  payload hashes, never API keys, invitation tokens or raw request payloads.
  Anonymous/authenticated direct access is revoked; exclude it from demo bundles.
- Immutable input guards, exact snapshot parent FKs, source FKs, one working/final
  revision, period overlap, last owner and receipt scopes are migration artifacts.
  Validate execution on an isolated populated database before production apply.

23.2 REQUEST/RESPONSE AND REVIEW DETAILS
- Require stable Idempotency-Key for new mutations and original quoted If-Match
  where documented. Authorization precedes receipt replay, then stale checks.
  Replay restores the accepted ETag/generation and uses Cache-Control: no-store.
  Resource counters, parent counters and case counters remain independent.
- ReviewRequest.current_revision is the CASE counter. FinalizeRequest uses
  case_input_revision; If-Match remains the resource counter. Positive decisions
  require frozen case sources to remain READY and unpurged at approval time.
- Binary evidence transport: authenticated PUT
  /api/v1/evidence/uploads/{eid}/content with declared Content-Type and stable
  Idempotency-Key; the server enforces the five-minute lease and exact hash/size.
  This supersedes exposing a Supabase signed-upload token: its provider TTL is
  longer than the required lease. Complete triggers fail-closed byte/MIME/parser
  checks. Authorized downloads remain short provider-signed URLs, at most 60s.
- Ordinary mutation JSON depth is 8. Global registry authoring allows depth 32 for
  nested reviewed JSON Schemas; all bodies remain <=1 MiB and schema references
  cannot fetch remote resources or form recursion cycles.
- Measurement deltas use the parent survey counter. Include server metadata
  last_survey_input_revision and deleted_at on delta rows, outside MCJ1 raw input.
  Correction copies get new IDs/local UUIDs and counters reset to 1.
- VIEWER institution discovery uses the latest released frozen profile. Before
  any release, list discovery returns only id, permission, membership_status and
  released_snapshot_id=NULL. Detail still requires an authorized released snapshot.
  Private evidence IDs/case navigation are redacted from released campus resources.
- Scoped operator digitization endpoint: POST
  /api/v1/admin/campuses/{cid}/boundaries, BoundaryCreate with source=
  OPERATOR_DIGITIZED, tenant-scoped BOUNDARY_REVIEW grant, Idempotency-Key and campus
  If-Match. It creates a pending candidate and contributor lineage. A different
  independent reviewer must approve it through the existing boundary review route.
- Privacy retained-item categories supported for explicit exceptions are EVIDENCE,
  ANNUAL_AUDIT, ANNUAL_RESULT, TREE_AUDIT and REVIEW_CASE. They map to the matching
  institution tables only, require requester ownership/membership lineage and an
  exactly matching approved policy category/basis. retention_until equals
  created_at + duration_days, or NULL with an explicit continuing hold basis.
  A planned personal purge target cannot be silently converted to an exception.

23.3 CAMPUS PROCESSING CONFIGURATION
MethodConfig gains optional campus_processing: CampusProcessingConfig or NULL.
It is required for mask/plot processing, not operational-only accounting methods.
CampusProcessingConfig required fields:
  mask_engine TEXT = REVIEWED_GEE_MASK_ASSET_V1
  mask_asset_id TEXT; mask_asset_update_time TEXT; mask_tree_value INTEGER
  mask_source_date DATE; mask_native_resolution_m Decimal
  mask_validation_evidence_id UUID; strata CampusStratum[]
CampusStratum required fields:
  name TEXT; geometry GeoJSON MultiPolygon; evidence_id UUID
  inclusion_probability Decimal in (0,1]; design_weight Decimal >0
The actual asset/date/update time/class must be reviewed and immutable. Clip its
eligible geometry to the approved boundary; never substitute a historical global
land-cover date or guessed vegetation threshold as a current campus measurement.
Sampling supports STRATIFIED_REVISITABLE with REVIEWED_FIXED_PLOT_WEIGHTS_V1,
actual measured clipped plot areas and reviewed spacing/edge rules. Failure to fit
the required plots is INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES, not fabricated sampling support.
Implemented model feature rules:
  aggregation = EXACT_PIXEL_INTERSECTION_EPSG6933_V1
  window_rule = REQUEST_INCLUSIVE_DATES_V1
  mask_rule = APPROVED_ELIGIBLE_MASK_V1
  fractional_pixel_rule = POSTGIS_GEODESIC_PIXEL_INTERSECTION_V1
Model domain schemas evaluate only these observed keys: domain=INSTITUTION,
campus_id, country, state, species_codes, pools, eligible_area_ha (decimal string),
assessment_month (integer), observation_start and observation_end. Do not claim
that a model supports a region/species/pool merely because a schema is permissive;
the independent validation artifact and real holdout metrics remain mandatory.

23.4 GEMINI, INGESTION AND ADVISORY
- LLM_PROVIDER=GEMINI; EMBEDDING_PROVIDER=GEMINI. Native allowlisted REST endpoint:
  https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/<pinned-model>:operation
  Use GEMINI_API_KEY; data-handling rules apply in code. Legacy LLM_BASE_URL,
  LLM_API_KEY and EMBEDDING_BASE_URL/API_KEY are unused compatibility settings.
- No default model ID/dimension or paid fallback. Match embedding model/dimension
  and reranker version to the independently activated corpus. Review current free
  tier terms, regions, model availability and quotas in the provider console.
- Free-form questions remain private. Both embedding and generation use the
  server's bounded nonidentifying intent phrase. Only actual aggregate quantities,
  allowed numeric/date/action constraints and approved public excerpts cross the
  provider boundary; no raw question, free-form note or tenant source document.
- RETRIEVAL policies commission UTF8_BYTES_V1, TT_PUBLIC_CORPUS_V1, actual chunk
  byte bounds (<=800, overlap < size), base_document_ids, allowed_source_hosts,
  exact embedding config and evaluated TFIDF_COSINE_V1 reranker with positive finite
  IDF weights and READY evaluation evidence. Public PDF URL ingestion validates
  HTTPS host/IP at every redirect and pins the verified public DNS address.
- The retrieval action_catalog maps supported action IDs to category, prerequisites
  and optional scenario_capability; category is a real inventory category.
  Rule ranking uses class order, known category share descending, action ID; unknown
  shares sort last. Recommendations are persisted with the answer in one publish
  transaction. Site checks are required unless support is actually established.
- PROVIDER/RETRIEVAL policy authoring/approval requires a global KNOWLEDGE_REVIEW
  grant. RETENTION uses PRIVACY_OPERATIONS. Approver differs from author. Terms/
  reranker evidence must reference actual READY global bytes. No evidence seeded.
- Invalid/truncated structured output produces an honest PARTIAL abstention.
  Quota failures and unknown HTTP outcomes preserve safe job errors. Durable
  operation checkpoints survive worker transaction rollback and prohibit duplicate
  unknown requests, including equivalent requests with a new idempotency key.

23.5 RESEARCH REFERENCES AND VALIDATION LIMITS
PDF language support: mount a licensed TrueType font and configure
INSTITUTION_REPORT_FONT_PATH / INSTITUTION_REPORT_FONT_SHA256. ReportLab's locked
shaping extra provides HarfBuzz bindings. Reject missing source glyphs or an
unconfigured Unicode font rather than silently replacing names with unreadable
characters. Validate actual Indic scripts and layout with the deployed font.
Font reference: https://docs.reportlab.com/reportlab/userguide/ch3_fonts/
Gemini structured JSON: https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/structured-output
Gemini embeddings: https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/embeddings
Gemini terms/pricing: https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing
Supabase upload TTL: https://supabase.com/docs/reference/python/storage-from-createsigneduploadurl
Geodesic area: https://postgis.net/docs/ST_Area.html
AES-GCM: https://cryptography.io/en/latest/hazmat/primitives/aead/
See INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md for exact commands and remaining live acceptance.
No SQL application, cloud integration or scientific validation is implied by the
offline suite and parser checks recorded in this implementation review.
23.6 FINAL BACKEND REVIEW — 21 OCTOBER 2026
- Staged geography_codes columns use TEXT[] in all four registries; the offline
  contract suite compares every table-card field against its physical SQL type.
  Corrected the generator's TEXT annotation handling without collapsing arrays.
- Dynamic institution OpenAPI routes declare UUID paths, repeated list filters,
  Firebase bearer auth, Idempotency-Key and original quoted If-Match headers.
- Feature-off discovery projects a frozen released profile even for an OWNER.
  Exact campus snapshot selection is honored; inventory campuses without stock
  assessments remain listed with NULL stock-related fields. Released projections
  clear private evidence/case references and bind the response context ID.
- Failures commit their durable job state before resource reconciliation. Recovery
  uses tenant -> resource -> job locks, rechecks terminal/latest source identity,
  and marks FAILURE_RECONCILED. Watchdog repairs crashes between those commits.
  Diagnostics record identifiers and safe error/type fields, never raw payloads.
- Satellite composites pin the actual recorded image IDs/acquisition timestamps;
  the per-sensor window bound is 10,000 images. Static-asset metadata is recorded.
  NISAR asset/type/bands are frozen at job acceptance; actual calibration, units,
  time coverage and cloud execution remain commissioning/validation requirements.
  Earth Engine references:
  https://developers.google.com/earth-engine/apidocs/ee-imagecollection-fromimages
  https://developers.google.com/earth-engine/apidocs/ee-imagecollection-filterdate
- Trends compare frozen campus/control context and, for stock/change/balance,
  boundary/mask support. Changed reporting boundaries block comparable deltas.
- Method publication verifies ranking/uncertainty/attribution rationale byte
  hashes and validation artifacts, compiles embedded policy schemas without
  remote references, and requires all referenced parameters in the same method.
  Advisor ordering uses the selected snapshot's frozen RULES_V1 ranking_config.
- See INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md for recorded offline checks and their limits.
  The operator sequence and remaining live gates are INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md.
- Personal mutations acquire identity locks before tenant locks; deletion takes
  the target identity exclusively before checking the approved complete plan.
  Personal question text is redacted from query rows and corresponding job input
  copies. Shared scientific hashes/history retain the disclosed policy basis.
  Confirm this ordering with real concurrent database transactions in staging.
- The institution Celery app does not change the farmer process's current app.
  Broker send timeouts are bounded; the outbox owns retrying and releases its
  batch after an outage. Only a currently leased worker projects RUNNING state.

23.7 ADDITIONAL PRODUCTION REVIEW CONTRACT — 21 OCTOBER 2026
This clarification supersedes conflicting older configuration/default wording.
- Both SQL domains, migration and restore connections verify TLS certificates and
  hostname. DATABASE_SSL_CA_FILE selects a mounted provider PEM; blank uses system
  trust. Validate the existing farmer connection in staging before release.
  Reference: https://docs.python.org/3/library/ssl.html#ssl.create_default_context
- Global-granted registry authoring and its source upload/scan/complete sequence
  require schema activation but remain available with tenant business writes off.
  Initial source evidence does not require a previously active methodology.
- Mutations lock identity FOR UPDATE before tenant scope. Authority approval joins
  membership atomically; bootstrap latches completion from historical joined_at.
  Deletion revokes operator grants; deleted actors cannot publish background jobs.
- Assessment discovery checks known schema properties only; final admission checks
  the complete observed domain including ordered dates and policy max_window_days.
  Configured default model engine/schema and optional quality-policy pins must agree.
  Species/equation choices derive from the exact accepted survey/method quality
  policy; ambiguous parameter selection remains explicit rather than latest-wins.
- Temporal revisit_matching_rule is STABLE_TREE_ID_AND_PLOT_SET_V1. Comparable
  stocks require matching plot_set_id and quality_policy_hash, plus observation
  window endpoints within seasonal_tolerance_days on a circular calendar year.
  Original provenance records these values. Missing provenance is NONCOMPARABLE.
  Field measurement height must equal the reviewed reference protocol height.
- Source overlap keys include campus_id. Nested allocations resolve independent
  of input row order and reject cycles. Negative factors are invalid. Parameter
  units must be explicit: rainwater intensity kWh/kL or kWh/m3; biogas LHV MJ/std_m3;
  efficiency fraction/1/dimensionless; displaced heat-fuel LHV MJ/<factor unit>.
- CSV commit revalidates source READY/hash, method, factors and activity schemas
  before inserting any rows; empty CSVs, duplicate mappings and future dates fail.
- Export advice uses the public projection and excludes private question/constraints.
  PDF metadata is invariant for identical frozen inputs/font/runtime. Byte checksums
  remain authoritative; changed font/runtime builds require acceptance again.
- EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE=1 and INSTITUTION_RASTER_CONCURRENCY=1 are supported fixed
  bounds. Chunk size is 4..800 UTF-8 bytes with no dropped characters; legacy env
  names CHUNK_SIZE_TOKENS/CHUNK_OVERLAP_TOKENS must match RETRIEVAL policy byte bounds.
  Policy/source changes require new document versions. Each chunk binds policy hash.
  RAG limits (vector/lexical <=20, rerank <=30, top_k <=6, RRF k >0) freeze into each
  job and its input hash. Configured batch/advisory limits are enforced at admission.
- Tree search matches stable_tag/species_code case-insensitively. sort supports
  stable_tag (default), -stable_tag, created_at, -created_at, with ID tie-breaking.
  Released tree search never reads live draft trees. Method category/domain filters
  and released mask boundary/plot-set filters are honored before pagination.
- scripts.institution.preflight is strictly offline: validates required fields,
  HTTPS URLs, local receipt key, font checksum and parseable optional CA PEM;
  --advisor additionally checks native Gemini model/provider configuration.
  This is configuration validation, not proof of live resource readiness.

================================================================================
22 OCTOBER 2026 AMENDMENT: SHARED BIOMASS AND SIMPLE DATABASE CONFIGURATION
================================================================================
This amendment governs new institution calculations and supersedes earlier
pretrained-model, environment UUID-pin and cloud receipt-key setup requirements.
Historical accepted jobs/results and optional legacy registry APIs are retained.

1. New institution tree audits call services.fusion_engine.run_fusion with their
   own accepted species/DBH/height/GPS observations, eligible geometry and dates.
   The shared GEE pipeline chooses its existing NISAR/Sentinel/GEDI fallbacks,
   computes Chave training targets, trains smileGradientTreeBoost per run
   (100 trees, shrinkage 0.05, sampling rate 0.7; at least 9 usable samples), and
   integrates the same 10 m prediction grid. No farmer result/credit is reused.
   Tree AGB_kg=0.0673*(rho*DBH_cm^2*height_m)^0.976; training t/ha=(kg/1000)/0.01.
   Carbon conversion uses the existing farmer constants 0.47 and 3.667. Farmer
   date defaults and behavior remain unchanged; institution uses accepted dates.
   Unquantified uncertainty/coverage stays unknown; standing stock is not annual
   removal. Institution baselines/attribution remain institution-owned.
2. BIOMASS_MODEL_VERSION, ACTIVE_ACCOUNTING_METHOD_VERSION, ACTIVE_CORPUS_VERSION,
   INSTITUTION_RECEIPT_KMS_KEY_ID and duplicate NISAR_ENABLED are retired/ignored.
   API preparation assigns the implementation ID; no trained model upload is
   needed for new institution jobs. Legacy artifact jobs keep their frozen path.
3. Forward phase 015 adds institution_calculation_settings (CURRENT config),
   institution_formula_values (editable numeric coefficient/unit/applicability),
   and institution_knowledge_sources (private public-guidance PDF locations).
   These tables are backend-only/RLS protected. Operators edit actual values in
   Supabase; code formulas remain allowlisted Python. No source PDF, approval
   ceremony, UUID entry or manual hash is required for administrator-maintained
   calculation constants. Scientific/site inputs remain explicit; missing values
   fail clearly and are never guessed. IDs/checksums/history are automatic.
   Fresh settings start empty; use the generated template and supply actual GPS,
   dimension, sampling, mask and stratum inputs. Prior real published settings
   are imported. New selections snapshot current values; accepted jobs, existing
   drafts and finalized results retain their selected/frozen configuration.
4. Public searchable licensed PDFs are uploaded under knowledge/ in the fixed
   private institution-evidence bucket. List enabled/public_licensed source rows,
   then run scripts.institution.sync_knowledge with a KNOWLEDGE_REVIEW operator.
   One durable job validates/copies bytes, extracts all configured sources,
   generates Gemini embeddings, and automatically activates the complete corpus
   only on successful fenced publication. Parser/source changes block activation.
   Latest compatible active corpus selection is automatic; edits require reindex.
   No tenant/private PDF is an eligible free-tier guidance source. No independent
   review or scientific validation is asserted for administrator-listed sources.
5. Invitation receipts use project-local AES-256-GCM through cryptography.AESGCM;
   random 12-byte nonces and AAD bind scope, request hash and expiry. Create one
   32-byte key using scripts.institution.create_receipt_key; preserve the private
   .institution-private/receipt.key across deployments and API instances. Key
   creation refuses overwrite; missing/wrong keys or tampered/expired ciphertext
   fail closed. No cloud KMS call or custom cipher exists. Older cloud-encrypted
   receipts require authorized invitation reissue after this change.
6. StockReconciliation is exactly the six BSDD fields: opening_quantity,
   purchased_quantity, closing_quantity, other_in_quantity, other_out_quantity,
   evidence_ids. Unrelated activity fields and recursive self-reference were
   removed from the compiled dictionary.
7. Manual steps: INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md; actual env sources:
   INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md; editable configuration:
   INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md; algorithm explanation:
   INSTITUTION_MANUAL_WORK.md. No live database/provider action or frontend
   modification is performed by preparing these backend files.
